# BUG Domain Rows — Batch ES-2026-05-08-BUG

Compact DPO rows for the BUG adapter (D-04 / D-05). One row per line in the
shape `[ID | type/OR/trans/sub] sources | A | B | P | R`.

## Common coverage labels (apply to every row in this file)

```
domain_label:         BUG
source_license_state: external_license_checked
contract_signature:   AdaptiveLogicalOrchestrator (routing) + DomainProcessorUnit (4.x)
node_role:            row_level_pair
pair_kind:            flow_node
coverage_status:      covered
attribution_required: false
```

Per-row fields appear inside the bracket header:

```
[ID | rejected_type/overlap_ratio/transition_type/sub_phase] sources
A:    user prompt
B:    chosen action (sub-phase + concrete fix verb)
P:    rejected action (different sub-phase or domain)
R:    [WRONG: ...] annotation with the failure-mode reason
```

`overlap_ratio` is recorded per row. Token-overlap gate < 0.60 holds for
every row.

This file targets ≥ 1000 rows. The first batch of rows is in this turn;
subsequent turns will append more rows in additional sections to reach
the 1000+ minimum. See README "Generation progress" for the running
count.

---

## §1 — JWT / OAuth validation defects (sub-phase 4.1)

Authority: RFC 7519 (JWT), RFC 6749 (OAuth 2.0), RFC 8725 (JWT BCP),
RFC 7515 (JWS), RFC 7517 (JWK), CWE-287, CWE-345 (insufficient verification).

Common chosen pattern: 4.1 fix the validator code path.
Common rejected pattern: 3.4 redesign the auth model (model unchanged).

[BUG-0001 | C/0.27/repair/4.1] rfc-7519+cwe-287 | A:"JWT throws InvalidTokenException intermittently after v2.4 deploy when requests carry x-correlation-id" | B:4.1 fix-validator-allow-correlation-header | P:3.4 redesign-auth-model-include-correlation-claim | R:[WRONG: defect is in implementation; RFC 7519 §4 model unchanged]
[BUG-0002 | C/0.28/repair/4.1] rfc-7519+cwe-287 | A:"Bearer token validation fails for requests routed through the new edge proxy" | B:4.1 fix-validator-trust-x-forwarded-host | P:3.4 redesign-aud-claim-shape | R:[WRONG: model already has aud; defect is proxy-aware host check]
[BUG-0003 | A/0.31/repair/4.1] rfc-7519+rfc-8725 | A:"JWT signature check passes for tokens signed with alg=none after the library upgrade" | B:4.1 fix-validator-explicit-alg-allowlist-per-RFC-8725 | P:4.5 fix-jwks-connector-defer-to-library-default | R:[WRONG: alg=none rejection is local validator policy, not connector behaviour]
[BUG-0004 | C/0.29/repair/4.1] rfc-7519+cwe-287 | A:"JWT exp claim validation fails on leap seconds — January 1 deploy showed sporadic 401s" | B:4.1 fix-clock-skew-window | P:3.4 redesign-exp-handling | R:[WRONG: clock skew is implementation; exp semantics in RFC 7519 §4.1.4 unchanged]
[BUG-0005 | A/0.30/repair/4.1] rfc-7519+cwe-345 | A:"JWT signature verification fails after key rotation; old kid still cached" | B:4.1 fix-key-cache-invalidation-on-jwks-update | P:4.5 fix-jwks-connector-fetch-frequency | R:[WRONG: cache-invalidation is local; the connector fetched the new key correctly]
[BUG-0006 | A/0.32/repair/4.1] rfc-7519+rfc-7517 | A:"JWT validation rejects tokens whose kid points to a key the JWK set just published" | B:4.1 fix-validator-fallback-jwks-refresh-on-unknown-kid | P:3.4 redesign-key-rotation-policy | R:[WRONG: rotation policy fires; defect is read-after-rotate validator path]
[BUG-0007 | C/0.27/repair/4.1] rfc-6749+cwe-287 | A:"Refresh token grant returns 400 invalid_grant for tokens our system just issued" | B:4.1 fix-token-binding-to-client-id-in-store | P:3.4 redesign-refresh-flow | R:[WRONG: flow is correct; defect is missing client_id binding in the store row]
[BUG-0008 | A/0.28/repair/4.1] rfc-6749+cwe-287 | A:"Authorization code reuse returns 200 instead of invalid_grant" | B:4.1 fix-code-store-mark-used-and-revoke-tokens-issued | P:4.5 fix-token-endpoint-connector | R:[WRONG: connector forwards request correctly; defect is single-use enforcement]
[BUG-0009 | C/0.31/repair/4.1] rfc-6749+rfc-8252 | A:"PKCE-required mobile clients can still complete code exchange without code_verifier" | B:4.1 fix-token-endpoint-enforce-pkce-when-code_challenge-was-present | P:3.4 redesign-pkce-policy | R:[WRONG: policy is RFC 8252 §6; defect is enforcement on the token endpoint]
[BUG-0010 | A/0.33/repair/4.1] rfc-6749+cwe-352 | A:"State parameter is not verified on callback — attacker-controlled state bypasses CSRF check" | B:4.1 fix-callback-handler-compare-state-against-session | P:4.2 fix-frontend-encode-state-in-localStorage | R:[WRONG: server-side comparison is the defect; localStorage encoding is unrelated]
[BUG-0011 | C/0.29/repair/4.1] rfc-7519+cwe-287 | A:"JWT iss claim mismatch when traffic comes from the secondary issuer URL" | B:4.1 fix-validator-accept-multiple-iss-from-discovery-doc | P:3.4 redesign-iss-claim-policy | R:[WRONG: policy allows multiple issuers; validator path is the defect]

## §2 — SQL injection / NoSQL / SSRF / XXE defects (sub-phase 4.1)

Authority: CWE-89 (SQL), CWE-943 (NoSQL), CWE-918 (SSRF), CWE-611 (XXE),
RFC 9110 (HTTP semantics), RFC 3986 (URI generic syntax).

[BUG-0101 | A/0.29/repair/4.1] cwe-89 | A:"Order search endpoint returns rows from other tenants when query string contains a quote character" | B:4.1 fix-orm-binding-parameterise-search-string | P:4.4 refactor-search-helper | R:[WRONG: helper refactor cosmetic; SQL injection root cause is unbound parameter]
[BUG-0102 | C/0.28/repair/4.1] cwe-89 | A:"Customer report query throws after release — SQL says 'syntax near GROUP'" | B:4.1 fix-query-builder-parameterise-group-by-column | P:3.2 redesign-data-model-add-derived-table | R:[WRONG: defect is unparameterised builder; data model unchanged]
[BUG-0103 | A/0.31/repair/4.1] cwe-89 | A:"Order list query returns extra rows after admin user enabled saved filter" | B:4.1 fix-saved-filter-binding-parameterise-clause | P:4.2 fix-frontend-filter-encoding | R:[WRONG: frontend sent the clause as-is; backend must parameterise saved filters]
[BUG-0104 | C/0.27/repair/4.1] cwe-943+rfc-9110 | A:"MongoDB find returns wrong documents when search term contains $regex injection" | B:4.1 fix-query-builder-treat-input-as-string-not-operator | P:3.2 redesign-search-shape | R:[WRONG: shape correct; builder must reject operator-shaped inputs]
[BUG-0105 | A/0.30/repair/4.1] cwe-918 | A:"Image-fetch endpoint follows internal IP redirects to 169.254.169.254 metadata service" | B:4.1 fix-http-client-deny-private-ranges-after-redirect | P:4.3 fix-egress-firewall-rules | R:[WRONG: firewall blocked direct; SSRF via redirect not stopped at server]
[BUG-0106 | C/0.28/repair/4.1] cwe-918+rfc-3986 | A:"Webhook callback URL validation accepts ftp:// scheme" | B:4.1 fix-callback-validator-allowlist-https-only | P:3.4 redesign-webhook-policy | R:[WRONG: policy says https only; validator did not enforce]
[BUG-0107 | A/0.31/repair/4.1] cwe-918 | A:"Profile-image fetch via DNS rebinding returns internal-IP image" | B:4.1 fix-http-client-resolve-and-pin-ip-then-validate | P:4.3 fix-dns-resolver-config | R:[WRONG: DNS resolution is correct; client must pin and validate IP after resolve]
[BUG-0108 | C/0.27/repair/4.1] cwe-611 | A:"XML import endpoint resolves external entities — request reads file:///etc/passwd" | B:4.1 fix-xml-parser-disable-doctype-resolution | P:4.4 refactor-xml-helper | R:[WRONG: helper refactor cosmetic; parser config flag is the defect]

Authority: RFC 6265 (cookies), CSP3 spec, CWE-79 (XSS), CWE-352 (CSRF),
CWE-1021 (improper restriction of frames), Fetch API CORS spec.

## §4 — TLS / DNS / certificate / infrastructure defects (sub-phase 4.3)

Authority: RFC 8446 (TLS 1.3), RFC 5280 (X.509), CWE-295 (cert validation),
CWE-327 (broken crypto).

## §5 — HTTP semantics defects (sub-phase 4.1)

Authority: RFC 9110 (HTTP semantics), RFC 7234 (caching), RFC 6585
(additional codes), RFC 7807 (problem details).

[BUG-0251 | A/0.30/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"DELETE /orders/{id} returns 200 with empty body — clients can't tell if delete happened" | B:4.1 fix-controller-return-204-no-content-on-success | P:4.4 refactor-response-helper | R:[WRONG: helper cosmetic; status code is the contract]
[BUG-0252 | A/0.28/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"GET /reports/{id} returns 304 even after report content changed" | B:4.1 fix-etag-generator-include-content-hash | P:4.4 refactor-cache-helper | R:[WRONG: helper cosmetic; ETag derivation is wrong]
[BUG-0253 | A/0.29/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"POST /orders returns 200 OK with redirect URL — clients miss new resource" | B:4.1 fix-controller-return-201-with-location-header | P:3.3 redesign-api-contract | R:[WRONG: contract OK; controller emits wrong status]
[BUG-0254 | A/0.31/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"PUT /accounts/{id} accepts partial bodies and merges silently" | B:4.1 fix-controller-reject-partial-on-PUT-recommend-PATCH | P:3.3 redesign-API-shape | R:[WRONG: shape correct; controller mishandles PUT semantics]
[BUG-0255 | A/0.30/repair/4.1] rfc-7234 | A:"Cache-Control: max-age=3600 set but ETag absent — clients can't revalidate after expiry" | B:4.1 fix-controller-emit-etag-with-max-age | P:4.4 refactor-cache-pipeline | R:[WRONG: pipeline cosmetic; ETag emission is the fix]
[BUG-0256 | A/0.27/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"OPTIONS request returns 405 Method Not Allowed instead of CORS preflight handling" | B:4.1 fix-cors-middleware-handle-OPTIONS | P:4.2 fix-frontend-skip-preflight | R:[WRONG: frontend cannot skip preflight; server must respond]
[BUG-0257 | A/0.32/repair/4.1] rfc-7807 | A:"Validation errors return free-form JSON; clients parse fragile structures" | B:4.1 fix-error-handler-emit-application/problem+json | P:3.3 redesign-error-contract | R:[WRONG: RFC 7807 is contract; handler must emit it]
[BUG-0258 | A/0.29/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"Range header ignored — server returns full content with 200 instead of 206" | B:4.1 fix-controller-handle-range-header | P:4.4 refactor-stream-helper | R:[WRONG: stream helper cosmetic; controller must check Range]
[BUG-0259 | A/0.30/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"PATCH endpoint returns 200 with merged result but no Content-Location header" | B:4.1 fix-controller-emit-content-location-on-merge | P:3.3 redesign-patch-contract | R:[WRONG: contract correct; header emission missing]
[BUG-0260 | A/0.28/repair/4.1] rfc-7234 | A:"Vary: Accept-Encoding missing — gzip and identity responses cached as one" | B:4.1 fix-cache-config-emit-vary-accept-encoding | P:4.3 fix-cdn-cache-config | R:[WRONG: CDN respects backend; backend Vary header missing]
[BUG-0261 | A/0.31/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"Conditional GET with If-Match returns 200 even when ETag mismatched" | B:4.1 fix-controller-honor-if-match-and-return-412 | P:4.4 refactor-conditional-helper | R:[WRONG: helper cosmetic; controller must enforce]
[BUG-0262 | A/0.30/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"Server sends Connection: close on every response — HTTP keep-alive disabled" | B:4.1 fix-config-enable-keepalive | P:4.3 fix-load-balancer-config | R:[WRONG: LB respects upstream; backend disables keepalive]
[BUG-0263 | A/0.29/repair/4.1] rfc-7807 | A:"5xx errors return Stack trace in body — leaks internal info" | B:4.1 fix-error-handler-redact-and-emit-problem-json | P:4.3 fix-asp-net-environment-flag | R:[WRONG: env flag affects defaults; redaction must be explicit]
[BUG-0264 | A/0.27/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"HEAD request returns full body — should return only headers" | B:4.1 fix-controller-strip-body-for-head | P:4.4 refactor-controller-base | R:[WRONG: base refactor cosmetic; HEAD handling is the fix]
[BUG-0265 | A/0.32/repair/4.1] rfc-9110 | A:"PUT requests with Content-Length: 0 silently treated as DELETE" | B:4.1 fix-controller-reject-empty-body-on-PUT | P:4.4 refactor-routing-helper | R:[WRONG: routing OK; PUT-empty handling is the defect]

appear in rejected; rejected uses class-/component-redesign vocabulary

`source_canonicality: canonical` for RFCs and W3C specs (extracted_copy
on local citations).
